## Sheet: CharacteristicBasic 260829-8295
| Technical Class | Description | Long Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| IR26641 | MOISTENER | Definition:An applicator, which slightly wets or damps objects or materials for moistening tape, envelopes, labels, etc., or for cooling or further processing.     Guidelines:If a specific qualifier cannot be determined, utilize the qualifier GENERIC. |
| IR26646 | MORTAR,REFRACTORY | Definition:A composition of finely ground refractory materials, marketed in either a wet or dry condition, which may require tempering with water to obtain the desired consistency and which is suitable for laying refractory brick and bonding them strongly upon drying, and heating at furnace temperatures.     Guidelines: |
| IR26648 | MOTHERBOARD | Definition:The main printed circuit board in an electronic device, which may contain sockets that accept additional boards.     Guidelines: |
| IR26067 | GRIP | Definition:(1) A cover fitted on an object or a handle used to prevent your hands from slipping. (2) An item shaped to fit the hand to provide a formed gripping surface e.g. to hold a pistol, revolver bayonet, etc. (3) Refer to GRIP CABLE, etc. for specific attributes.     Guidelines:If a specific qualifier cannot be determined, utilize the qualifier GENERIC. |
| IR26077 | GUIDE | Definition:A device, such as a rail, tab, or bar used for directing or regulating a motion or an operation. Refer to GUIDE VALVE, etc. for specific attributes.     Guidelines:If a specific qualifier cannot be determined, utilize the qualifier GENERIC. |
| IR25898 | FLANGE,PIPE | Definition:An item that is solid or split into halves, having a threaded or unthreaded hole for the purpose of connecting or retaining a pipe, tube, or hose fitting.     Guidelines:If description contains the words PIPE, WELD NECK, with a CLASS or LB rating, utilize the qualifier PIPE. |
| IR26446 | LENS,SAFETY EQUIPMENT | Definition:A transparent or opaque optical device designed for the protection of the eyes such as lenses for goggles, welding helmet, etc.     Guidelines:If description contains the words safety, welding, or hood, utilize the qualifier SAFETY EQUIPMENT. |
| IR26451 | LEVER,GENERIC | Definition:A rigid bar or a projecting handle designed to pivot to adjust or operate a mechanism, or to move or lift a load at one end by applying force to the other end.     Guidelines:If a specific qualifier cannot be determined, utilize the qualifier GENERIC. |
| IR26460 | LIGHT | Definition:Any device serving as a source of illumination.     Guidelines:If a specific qualifier cannot be determined, utilize the qualifier GENERIC. |
| IR27307 | SET,GENERIC | Definition:More than one item, of the same kind, may have different sizes, packaged together, and is so used.     Guidelines:If a specific qualifier cannot be determined, utilize the qualifier GENERIC. |
| IR27712 | TEE,SOLDER JOINT | Definition:It is a fitting that resembles the shape of the letter T, having three connections, with the branch connection fixed at 90 degrees to the run. Connections are secured by soldering and are used to join tubes and pipes and sweated connections, generally it is used for copper fittings in a plumbing/refrigeration applications.     Guidelines:If description contains the words solder, or sweat, utilize the qualifier SOLDER JOINT. |
| IR27713 | TEE,STREET | Definition:It is a fitting that resembles the shape of the letter T, one connection is 90 degrees to the run. The run has female pipe threads on one end and male pipe threads on the other end, and the branch has female pipe threads; may have reducing sizes.     Guidelines:If description contains the words street, utilize the qualifier STREET. |
| IR27715 | TEE,TUBE ADAPTOR | Definition:It is a fitting that resembles the shape of the letter T, having three connections, with the branch connection fixed at 90 degrees to the run. It must have different tube-type end connections, which may be of the same or different sizes, used to connect tubes.     Guidelines:If description contains the words female branch, female run, male branch, male run, or tube x pipe, utilize the qualifier TUBE ADAPTOR. |
| IR27605 | SUGAR,REFINED | Definition:A sweet crystalline or powdered substance, white when pure, consisting of sucrose, obtained mainly from sugar cane and sugar beets, is important as a source of dietary carbohydrate, as a sweetener and preservative for other foods.     Guidelines: |
| IR27607 | SUIT,GENERIC | Definition:A two-piece, outer garment, specifically designed, as a set, consisting of a coat with trousers or a skirt, may also be a costume for a special activity.     Guidelines:If a specific qualifier cannot be determined, utilize the qualifier GENERIC. |
| IR27610 | SUPPORT,GENERIC | Definition:A rigid structural item, designed to hold a part or group of parts in its proper position bearing, the stress imposed by the parts.     Guidelines: |
| IR27611 | SUPPRESSOR,GENERIC | Definition:An electronic item of various construction, specifically designed to protect electronic/electrical components from damage against voltage surges, by absorbing or consuming such surges.     Guidelines:If a specific qualifier cannot be determined, utilize the qualifier GENERIC. |
| IR27620 | SWAB,GENERIC | Definition:A composition of absobent synethetic yarns, or other suitable materials.     Guidelines: |
| IR27622 | SWEATER,GENERIC | Definition:An outer garment, usually knitted or crocheted, to be worn, covering the top portion of the human body.     Guidelines: |
| IR27624 | SWEETENER,NON SUGAR | Definition:Something added to foods or beverages to make them taste sweeter.     Guidelines: |
| IR27630 | SWITCH,LIMIT | Definition:A switch designed with a quick acting mechanism, highly delicate, actuated by small movement, having a definite travel rating and mechanical pressure.     Guidelines:If description contains the word limit, utilize the qualifier LIMIT. |
| IR26345 | ISOLATOR,GENERIC | Definition:A device, which allows radio frequency energy to pass in one direction with very little power loss, but blocks the flow of energy in the other direction by means of multiple WAVE-polarization or an integral dissipative load. Refer to ISOLATOR ELECTRICAL, etc. for specific attributes.     Guidelines:If a specific qualifier cannot be determined, utilize the qualifier GENERIC. |
| IR26348 | JACK,HYDRAULIC HAND | Definition:A lifting device consisting of a ram with a hand-operated hydraulically actuated pump, which may be combined on a single base, in a common body or be of the remote control type, with hydraulic force actuating the ram in one direction only.     Guidelines: |
| IR26351 | JACKET,GENERIC | Definition:A short, usually hip-length or waist-length coat, which is sometimes warn as part of a suit, with long sleeves, pockets, lapels, and fastens at the front with buttons or a zipper. Refer to JACKET OVERALL, etc. for specific attributes.     Guidelines:If a specific qualifier cannot be determined, utilize the qualifier GENERIC. |
| IR27774 | TIP,SOLDERING | Definition:A metallic item designed to be attached to the end of a soldering gun or iron for the purpose of transmitting heat to the item being soldered. It may include an integral electrical heating element. It may be a nonmetallic item for de-soldering.     Guidelines:If description contains the words solder or soldering, utilize the qualifier SOLDERING. |
| IR27775 | TIP,TORCH | Definition:Is a metal device in the form of a short tube, which is attached to the outlet end of a welding or cutting torch.     Guidelines:If description contains the words torch, welding or Victor, utilize the qualifier TORCH. |
| IR27779 | TOBACCO,CHEWING | Definition:Any of various plants of the genus Nicotiana, especially N. tabacum, native to tropical America and widely cultivated for their leaves, which are used primarily for chewing.     Guidelines: |
| IR27780 | TOBACCO,SMOKING | Definition:Any of various plants of the genus Nicotiana, especially N. tabacum, native to tropical America and widely cultivated for their leaves, which are used primarily for smoking.     Guidelines:PIPE |
| IR25075 | BORESCOPE,GENERIC | Definition:A rigid tube with an eyepiece on one end and an imaging lens on the other designed for armament barrel adjustment or inspection work.     Guidelines:If a specific qualifier cannot be determined, utilize the qualifier GENERIC. |
| IR27437 | SOLUTION,CALCIUM | Definition:A solution containing a specific ion (Ca) in a predetermined concentration.     Guidelines: |
| IR27441 | SOLUTION,COPPER | Definition:A solution containing a specific ion (Cu) in a predetermined concentration.     Guidelines: |
| IR27447 | SOLUTION,MAGNESIUM | Definition:A solution containing a specific ion (Mg) in a predetermined concentration.     Guidelines: |
| IR27449 | SOLUTION,MERCURY | Definition:A solution containing a specific ion (Fe) in a predetermined concentration.     Guidelines: |
| IR27455 | SOLUTION,SELENIUM | Definition:A solution containing a specific ion (Se) in a predetermined concentration.     Guidelines: |
| IR27461 | SOLUTION,THIOSULPHATE | Definition:A solution containing a specific ion (S2O3) in a predetermined concentration.     Guidelines: |
| IR27466 | SOLUTION,ZINC | Definition:A solution containing a specific ion (Zn) in a predetermined concentration.     Guidelines: |
| IR27471 | SOUP,GENERIC | Definition:A liquid food prepared from meat, fish, or vegetables, combined with various other ingredients, it may contain solid pieces.     Guidelines: |
| IR26592 | METAL SALT,GENERIC | Definition:A chemical compound formed by replacing all or part of the hydrogen ions of an acid with metal ions.     Guidelines: |
| IR26616 | METALS,TITANIUM | Definition:An electropositive material/element that is formed with alloys such as copper, zinc, brass, etc., which is generally good conductor of heat and electricity.     Guidelines: |
| IR27688 | TAPE,FISH | Definition:A hand tool which consisting of a loop of wire or wire hook to reach through locations that are inaccessible to slip over or around electrical leads and pull them through close places in electronic equipment. Excludes probes and grip type cable pullers.     Guidelines:If description contains the words fish or conduit, utilize the qualifier FISH. |
| IR27699 | TAR,COAL | Definition:A black viscous liquid prepared from crude gashouse, coke-oven, and/or water-gas tars, is designed for use for road construction for prime coat, surface treatment, seal coat, penetration, and repairing roofs or as filler for an expansion joint.     Guidelines: |
| IR27702 | TEA,GENERIC | Definition:Is an eastern Asian evergreen shrub or small tree having fragrant, nodding, cup-shaped white flowers and glossy leave. The young, dried leaves of this plant, prepared by various processes and used to make a hot beverage.     Guidelines: |
| IR25123 | BRUSH,SANITARY | Definition:An implement consisting of bristles set into a long handle designed for cleaning washbasins, toilet bowls, bedpans, etc.     Guidelines: |
| IR25104 | BREAD ROLL,GENERIC | Definition:A ready-to-eat unit of bread, of various shapes, designed for individual serving.     Guidelines: |
| IR24912 | BAR,ROUND CORED | Definition:A ferrous or nonferrous metallic material, produced as a solid section in straight lengths or short bar-like forms of cored or solid cross section. It must be hollow and has a circular cross-section.     Guidelines:If description contains the words round w/ cored, or ID, utilize the qualifier ROUND CORED. |
| IR24914 | BAR,WRENCH | Definition:A hand tool designed to fit through the hole in the body of a socket wrench to provide leverage and may include a means of preventing the socket wrench from sliding off.     Guidelines:If description contains the words wrench, Snap-On, or proto, utilize the qualifier WRENCH. |
| IR26556 | MASK,RESPIRATOR | Definition:A protective mask with a filter designed to protect the face and lungs against poisonous gases and aids in breathing.     Guidelines: |
| IR26562 | MAT,FLOOR | Definition:A piece of floor covering of definite size and shape made of a variety of materials.     Guidelines: |
| IR26565 | MATCH,GENERIC | Definition:A narrow piece, usually of wood or cardboard, coated on one end with a compound that ignites when scratched against a rough or chemically treated surface.     Guidelines:If a specific qualifier cannot be determined, utilize the qualifier GENERIC. |
| IR26422 | LANYARD,SAFETY INDUSTRIAL | Definition:A flexible line designed to secure a body harness to a lifeline or directly to a point of anchorage.     Guidelines:FALL ARREST; etc. |
| IR26426 | LATCH,GENERIC | Definition:(1) A device designed for mating mechanical parts to engage to fasten but usually not to lock something. (2) A securing device for a door, window or gate that can be lowered or slid into a groove. (3) A spring-loaded door lock that can only be opened from the outside with a key.     Guidelines:If a specific qualifier cannot be determined, utilize the qualifier GENERIC. |
| IR26432 | LEAD,ELECTRICAL | Definition:A definite length of one-conductor electrical wire, wire braid, or other conductive material, except cable or cord, one or both ends of which are processed or terminated and may be of any size or shape, insulated or un-insulated.     Guidelines: |
| IR24897 | BAND,POLE | Definition:A metallic clamp, which encircles a pole, used for lifting objects.     Guidelines:If description contains the word pole, utilize the qualifier POLE. |
| IR24899 | BAND,SWEAT | Definition:A narrow strip of fabric or leather sewn inside the crown of a hat used as protection against sweat, or a band of material worn around the head or wrist to absorb sweat.     Guidelines:If description contains the word sweat, utilize the qualifier SWEAT. |
| IR26355 | JAR,BATTERY | Definition:A container specifically designed to in-close one or more electrode assemblies and the required electrolyte for a battery, which may also be compartmented.     Guidelines:If description contains the word battery, utilize the qualifier BATTERY. |
| IR26110 | HANGER,PIPE AND CONDUIT | Definition:A metallic or nonmetallic item, designed to suspend, retain, and position conduit, pipes, tubes, etc. in a structure.     Guidelines:If description contains the words pipe, constant, conduit w/o fixture, clevis, or minerallac, utilize the qualifier PIPE AND CONDUIT. |
| IR25038 | BOAT,GENERIC | Definition:A small vessel designed to sail on water or a dish (often boat-shaped) for serving gravy or sauce.     Guidelines:If a specific qualifier cannot be determined, utilize the qualifier GENERIC. |
| IR25040 | BODY,GENERIC | Definition:(1) The main central or principal part, as the nave of a church. (2) The bed or box of a vehicle on or in which the load is placed. (3) The enclosed or partly enclosed part of an automobile. (4) A held-together collection or group of physical objects. Refer to BODY VALVE, etc. for specific attributes.     Guidelines:If a specific qualifier cannot be determined, utilize the qualifier GENERIC. |
| IR25041 | BODY,TOWER | Definition:An open frame structure (tower) which is high in proportion to its lateral dimensions and has more than a single footing or mounting base, for supporting items such as transmission lines etc.     Guidelines:If description contains the word tower, utilize the qualifier TOWER. |
| IR25049 | BOLT,EYE | Definition:A fastener consisting of a threaded pin or shaft of one nominal diameter designed with a ring-shaped head.     Guidelines:If description contains the words eye w/o double arming, utilize the qualifier EYE. |
| IR25822 | FACE,SIGN | Definition:An item designed as a panel and which is inserted into the sign frame and is marked with a legend, symbol, or combination of both.     Guidelines:If description contains the word sign, utilize the qualifier SIGN. |
| IR26382 | KEYBOARD,GENERIC | Definition:A set of keys mounted on a panel used on a computer, typewriter, piano etc.     Guidelines:If a specific qualifier cannot be determined, utilize the qualifier GENERIC. |
| IR26383 | KEYPAD,GENERIC | Definition:An input device, sometimes part of a standard computer keyboard, consisting of a separate grid of numerical and function keys arranged for efficient data entry.     Guidelines:If a specific qualifier can not be determined, utilize the qualifier GENERIC. |
| IR26391 | KIT | Definition:Equipment containing a collection of specific items or a set of parts that are to be assembled. e.g. Model airplane kit.     Guidelines:If a specific qualifier cannot be determined, utilize the qualifier GENERIC. |
| IR26804 | PAD,CONCRETE | Definition:A slab of concrete used as a base or foundation for pad mounted equipment.     Guidelines:If description contains the words transformer, reinforced, cement or Xfmr., utilize the qualifier CONCRETE. |
| IR25908 | FLASK,INSULATED | Definition:A vessel that is double insulated by means of foam or gas and manufactured to hold dry ice, solvent bath, liquid air, and liquid nitrogen.     Guidelines: |
| IR25914 | FLOAT | Definition:A hand-tool manufactured to smoothen a cement surface. (2) An object that remains suspended within or on the surface of a fluid without sinking and can be attached to a lever to regulate the water level in a tank. (3) A large anchored buoy, often equipped with lights or bells that serves as a guide or warning to ships.     Guidelines:If a specific qualifier cannot be determined, utilize the qualifier GENERIC. |
| IR26337 | INTERLOCK,GENERIC | Definition:A device used for connecting two components in order for the individual components to affect each other in motion or operation e.g. systems to lock into one another or coordinate in such a way that all parts work together effectively.     Guidelines:If a specific qualifier cannot be determined, utilize the qualifier GENERIC. |

| IR27316 | SHAFT,PUMP | Definition:A shaft designed for a specific pump, may be supplied bare or as an assembly of shaft, bearings, seals, etc.     Guidelines:If description contains the words pump or line-shaft, utilize the qualifier PUMP. |
| IR27329 | SHEET,BED | Definition:A mattress covering for a bed specifically made according to mattress standard sizes, fitted or flat.     Guidelines: |
| IR26285 | INORGANIC BASE,POTASSIUM | Definition:A compound originated from a series of mineral and metal substances, and a high concentration of hydrogen ions combined with a soft silvery white reactive element of the alkali metal group, commonly found combined in minerals used as a coolant in nuclear reactors, although its compounds are used also in fertilizers.     Guidelines: |
| IR27364 | SHREDDING MACHINE,GENERIC | Definition:A machine, designed to shred large quantities of various materials. Excluding, PAPER.     Guidelines: |

| IR24825 | ALARM,AUDIBLE VISUAL | Definition:An electrical item which may be part of or coupled to equipment to give automatic audible and visual warning of failure of the equipment or of receipt of a signal.     Guidelines: |

| IR27573 | STOVE,GENERIC | Definition:An item, designed to furnish heat for cooking food, or heating required for specialized industrial purposes, using electricity or a fuel.     Guidelines:If a specific qualifier cannot be determined, utilize the qualifier GENERIC. |
| IR27580 | STRAP,PIPE | Definition:A thin flat strip of flexible material, such as metal, plastic etc., designed for fastening or supporting a pipe, may be equipped with a fastening device for binding or securing the objects.     Guidelines:If description contains the word pipe, utilize the qualifier PIPE. |
| IR27583 | STRAP,WRENCH | Definition:A hand tool, with handle and flexible strap, designed to grip an item, like an oil filter, with the strap and apply a rotational force with the handle.     Guidelines:If description contains the word wrench, utilize the qualifier WRENCH. |
| IR27588 | STRIP,GENERIC | Definition:A piece of any material, relatively narrow compared to its length, normally of equal width and thickness.     Guidelines:If a specific qualifier cannot be determined, utilize the qualifier GENERIC. |
| IR27591 | STRIP,WEAR | Definition:A narrow strip of material used as a wearing surface in various applications, e.g. slat chain conveyor and chain conveyor systems.     Guidelines:If the description contains the text wear, lubricating or self lubricating, utilize the qualifier, WEAR. |
| IR27340 | SHIELD,EYE | Definition:An item designed to eliminate infiltration of light to the eye and to protect a traumatized condition of the eye.     Guidelines: |
| IR26630 | MILK,EVAPORATED | Definition:Unsweetened milk concentrated by partial evaporation from which 60% of the water has been removed.     Guidelines: |
| IR26633 | MIRROR,GENERIC | Definition:A coated, polished or smooth metal surface, or high quality amalgam-coated glass, for reflecting an image or light rays without appreciable diffusion.     Guidelines:If a specific qualifier cannot be determined, utilize the qualifier GENERIC. |
| IR26885 | PENCIL,MECHANICAL | Definition:A narrow generally cylindrical mechanical holder for writing, drawing, or marking containing a replaceable rod of graphite, colored wax, slate or similar substance in various sizes.     Guidelines:If description contains the word mechanical or clutch, utilize the qualifier MECHANICAL. |
| IR26549 | MARKER,IDENTIFICATION | Definition:A flexible item designed to be affixed to an object by adhesion, with any configuration and preprinted with legendary characters, symbol(s), letter(s), numeral(s), or coding signification and may be coated with a reflector substance.     Guidelines:It does not contain data, which indicates contents, destination or similar signification. May be in roll form if perforations or markings are provided for detaching items of a specific length. |
| IR26317 | INSERT,COUPLING | Definition:An item specifically designed to be inserted between the driving and driven members of a coupling to allow for slight misalignment of the shafts.     Guidelines:If the description contains the text Tire, Pin and coupling, and indicates it is an insert for flexible couplings, utilize the qualifier, COUPLING. This excludes Wellman Bibby and Spider couplings. |
| IR26331 | INSULATOR,GENERIC | Definition:An item made of nonconductive material, such as natural occurring mineral mica, vitrified inorganic materials produced by means of heat (ceramic materials), or material, which are electrically rated by government or industry standards or specifications.     Guidelines:If a specific qualifier cannot be determined, utilize the qualifier GENERIC. |

Guidelines: |

| IR26324 | INSERT,THREADING | Definition:A cylindrical item, which may have wrenching and/or driving facilities and/or shoulders, which is inserted in a drilled or a pre-tapped hole, or molded or pressed into soft material to reduce the size of a tapped hole, or to provide threads for adding strength, and/or to prevent stripping. Guidelines:If description contains the word step, utilize the qualifier STEP. |

| IR26447 | LENTIL,GENERIC | Definition:There are three main varieties of lentils. | IR25584 | CURRANT,GENERIC | Definition:There are two distinctly different fruits called currant. (1) The first - resembling a tiny, dark raisin - is the seedless, dried ZANTE GRAPE. Its name comes from its place of origin - Corinth, Greece. In cooking, this type of currant (like raisins) is used mainly in baked goods. (2) The second type of currant is a tiny berry related to the gooseberry. There are black, red and white currants. The black ones are generally used for preserves, syrups and liqueurs (such as CASSIS), while the red and white berries are good for out-of-hand eating and such preparations as the famous French preserve BAR-LE-DUC and (using the red currants) CUMBERLAND SAUCE.     Guidelines: |

| IR26705 | NOODLES,DRY | Definition:A food made from formed units of dough (flour or semolina) containing eggs. The units are usually in the form of flat narrow strips. The main difference between noodles and MACARONI or SPAGHETTI is that, in addition to flour and water, noodles contain eggs or egg yolks. Noodles can be cut into flat, thick or thin strips of various lengths. They may also be cut into squares. A wide variety of noodles is available in markets, including those enriched with vitamins and minerals, and colored noodles (red tinted with tomato paste or beet juice and green with spinach).     Guidelines: |
| IR26710 | NUT,ADAPTOR | Definition:A nut that is used to secure or position a bearing.     Guidelines:If the description contains the text Bearing, Nut and/or Sleeve Adaptor, utilize the qualifier ADAPTOR. |

| IR26720 | NUT,SLOTTED | Definition:A nut having a flat bearing surface and 6 or 8 flat sides with slots in its upper portion. It is designed to provide positive locking when used with a solid or split (cotter) pin.     Guidelines:If description contains the words slotted, castle, or castellated, utilize the qualifier SLOTTED. |
| IR26731 | OIL,GEAR | Definition:A petroleum or synthetic product with load-carrying ingredients, especially prepared for lubrication of various type gears, such as worm gears, spiral bevel type gears, hypoid gears, transmissions, etc.     Guidelines: |

| IR25433 | COLUMN,ANALYSIS | Definition:An analyzer, with a cylindrical pillar, designed to analyze the content of gases or liquids, or to preserve, absorb or separate gases.     Guidelines: |
| IR25434 | COLUMN,GENERIC | Definition:An upright structure of various construction, designed to support a heavy load, e.g. roof, ceiling, etc. (Refer to COLUMN; PUMP, etc. for specific attributes.)     Guidelines:If a specific qualifier cannot be determined, utilize the qualifier GENERIC. |
| IR25440 | COMPASS,DRAFTING | Definition:A V-shaped device for describing circles or circular arcs and for taking measurements, consisting of a pair of rigid, end-hinged legs, one of which is equipped with a pen, pencil, or other marker and the other with a sharp point providing a pivot about which the drawing leg is turned. Also referred to as a pair of compasses.     Guidelines:If description contains the words bow, divider, pen, draft or draw, utilize the qualifier DRAFTING. |

Guidelines:If description contains the words particle, blast, bead, shell, or powder, utilize the qualifier ABRASIVE. |
| IR27894 | TURMERIC,GENERIC | Definition:A cultivated tropical plant ground dried rhizome of the turmeric plant used as seasoning or source of a condiment and a yellow dye.     Guidelines: |

Guidelines:If a specific qualifier cannot be determined, utilize the qualifier GENERIC. |
| IR27905 | UNION,GENERIC | Definition:A straight fitting, designed to connect parts in-line, such as pipes or rods.     Guidelines:If a specific qualifier cannot be determined, utilize the qualifier GENERIC. |
| IR27908 | UNION,TUBE | Definition:A straight tubefitting, designed to join or disconnect two lengths of tubes or fittings without disturbing the rest of the pipeline, with end connections to accommodate a tube.     Guidelines:If description contains the words tube w/o bulkhead, compression or flare, utilize the qualifier TUBE. |
| IR30018 | WARDROBE,GENERIC | Definition:A wardrobe or armoire is a standing closet used for storing clothes. The earliest wardrobe was a chest, and it was not until some degree of luxury was attained in regal palaces and the castles of powerful nobles that separate accommodation was provided for the apparel of the great. The name of wardrobe was then given to a room in which the wall-space was filled with closets and lockers, the drawer being a comparatively modern invention. |
| IR30019 | SLIPPERS,GENERIC | Definition:Slippers are light footwear that are easy to put on and off and are intended to be worn indoors, particularly at home. |

| IR27988 | WEBBING,GENERIC | Definition:Strong, narrow, closely woven fabric designed, for bearing weight, used especially for straps, harnesses, or upholstery.     Guidelines:If a specific qualifier cannot be determined, utilize the qualifier GENERIC. |
| IR27990 | WEDGE,VALVE | Definition:A part for a valve, designed to match its seat, to regulate the flow of liquids or gases through the valve.     Guidelines:If description contains the words valve, vlv or prv, utilize the qualifier VALVE. |
| IR27998 | WHEEL,ABRASIVE | Definition:A grinding wheel designed from a rigid piece of abrasive, with mounting facilities for mounting on a grinding machine, tool chuck or mandrel, may be without mounting facilities to be secured in a wheel holder by means of spring clamps.     Guidelines:Grinding, abrasive, flap, grind-o-flex, or flapper wheel. |
| IR28002 | WHEEL,WIRE | Definition:A wheel shaped item, with a fill section of wire material, of various configurations, designed for roughing, buffing and/or polishing surfaces, with mounting facilities, attaching to a rotary power-operated tool or machine.     Guidelines:If description contains the words wire, brush, or cup, utilize the qualifier WIRE. |
| IR28047 | WRENCH,TAP | Definition:A hand tool, designed to hold and apply rotary force to taps and reamers, may have a ratchet mechanism.     Guidelines:If description contains the word tap, utilize the qualifier TAP. |
| IR29002 | TORQUE CONVERTER,GENERIC | Definition:An item designed to transmit rotary motion from a driving member to a driven member by transfer of the kinetic energy of a fluid and through a reaction member(s), provide automatic selection of optimum torque conversion ratio with a return to the 1-to-1 state at the earliest possible moment. May have flywheel features and facilities for direct drive.     Guidelines: |
| IR29757 | DRIVE,FLUID | Definition:An hydrodynamic (liquid in motion) power transmission drive. A power transmission coupling that provides a smooth start, consisting of two separate turbines that rotate on the same axis in a surrounding liquid, such that the turbine connected to the engine drives the turbine connected to the transmission.     Guidelines:If description contains the words fluid clutch, soft start, Voith shaft coupling, utilize the Object DRIVE and Qualifier FLUID. |
| IR25687 | DISPLACER,GENERIC | Definition:A funnel-type component on an apparatus used for solution displacement, or a component used in a switch to determine the linear and proportional relationship between a spring tension and water level.     Guidelines:If a specific qualifier cannot be determined, utilize the qualifier GENERIC. |
| IR25695 | DOCKING STATION,COMPUTER | Definition:A small cabinet to which a laptop or notebook computer can be attached and used as a desktop computer, usually having a connector for externally connected devices, such as hard drives or scanners, and ports that can be linked to components such as a keyboard, monitor, and printer.     Guidelines: |
| IR25101 | BRANDING IRON,GENERIC | Definition:An implement, usually manufactured with alphabetic letters attached to a rod designed to brand or make a stigma on livestock.     Guidelines: |
| IR25103 | BREAD,GENERIC | Definition:A food type made from flour or meal by moistening, kneading, and baking.     Guidelines: |
| IR25163 | CABLE,COMPENSATING | Definition:Two or more pairs, solid or stranded, individually insulated conductors, of a specific type, in a common covering, used for thermocouples connecting to cold junction boxes.     Guidelines:If the description contains the text Type K, Type R, Type J together with cable and indicates it is a cable used to connect Thermocouples to Cold Junction boxes, utilize the qualifier COMPENSATING. |
| IR25164 | CABLE,ELECTRICAL | Definition:One or more, solid or stranded, insulated conductors of various design, may have a common covering, or metallic covering, shield, or outer conductor, to provide an electrical connection between electrical components/systems.     Guidelines:If description contains the words SWG, MCM, KCMIL, KV, distribution, URD or underground, utilize the qualifier ELECTRICAL. This is for 16MMSQ and larger for smaller than 16MMSQ utilize WIRE ELECTRICAL. |
| IR25747 | EJECTOR,GENERIC | Definition:An assembled unit consisting of one or more jet ejectors with inter-condensers or after-condensers designed to maintain a partial vacuum by the removal of air and other non-condensable gases from a closed vessel without a resulting loss of vapor to the atmosphere. (2) A device in a gun for emitting empty shells, or in a seat for ejecting.     Guidelines:If a specific qualifier cannot be determined, utilize the qualifier GENERIC. |
| IR25749 | ELBOW,GENERIC | Definition:A fitting that forms an angle of less than 180 degrees from the straight flow.     Guidelines:If a specific qualifier cannot be determined, utilize the qualifier GENERIC. |
| IR25936 | FORK,PITCH | Definition:A long-handled gardening tool with two or more curved prongs for lifting solids e.g. hay, grass, etc.     Guidelines:If description contains the words pitch, hay, coal or refuse, utilize the qualifier PITCH. |
| IR25937 | FORK,TABLE | Definition:An eating utensil having two or more prongs.     Guidelines: |
| IR25160 | CABINET,GENERIC | Definition:An item consisting of an in-closed frame, provided with a combination of drawer(s), shelves, panels, etc. to keep articles, may have door(s), to secure contents or give access to the enclosure.     Guidelines:If a specific qualifier cannot be determined, utilize the qualifier GENERIC. |

| IR27734 | TERRINE,GENERIC | Definition:A dish, pan or jar, originally of earthenware, such as those which various dishes are cooked and served in and sold with its contents.     Guidelines: |
| IR25334 | CHISEL,COLD HAND | Definition:A hand held metal cutting tool, of steel, having a straight, double beveled symmetrical cutting edge on one end, with the opposite end used as a striking head.     Guidelines:Cold or hand; etc. |
| IR25700 | DOSIMETER,GENERIC | Definition:An instrument used for measuring and that indicates the amount of x-rays or radiation absorbed in a given period.     Guidelines:If a specific qualifier cannot be determined, utilize the qualifier GENERIC. |
